Under Article 1 of the contract, the agreed parties have secured the rights and freedoms that this contract has committed to all those in the territory of sovereignty. Therefore, in the proceedings of the Strasbourg Court, the idea of positive obligations to the state to carry out specific actions in order to protect the rights of individuals is included in the opinion of the Court of Strasbourg. According to the European court, the positive obligations require that national authorities take reasonable and appropriate measures to protect the individual's rights. tbmm-2014-02-20sit02spe0081par0006	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of myself on the majority of the General Law Proposal for the removal of the High Criminal Courts established when Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Act, 560, and the amendments to various Laws. I salute your esteemed delegation and my beloved nation. Dear lawmakers, as it is known, the purpose of criminal prosecution is to achieve material truth without causing human rights violations. The constitutional principle mentioned above during criminal investigations and prosecutions must be considered, and the restrictions on basic rights and freedoms must be reasonable and measured. In this regard, the proposal of the law makes very important arrangements. The proposal of the law allows the removal of severe criminal courts established under Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law to take one step further into the creation of the ideal legal system. The courts, known as state security courts in the first place, have been removed by the Constitutional Amendment in 2004, in the time of the EC PARTI rule, which poses a problem in our democracy, fundamental rights and freedoms and the state of law. A number of crimes that entered the jurisdiction of the courts were left to the criminal courts of the special authority, which was established under Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law. Under the proposal, the special official criminal courts established by Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law are being removed, and the files that these courts are looking at are handed over to heavy criminal courts. It is an important step in implementing the rule of law state. It is not right in the legal state to adopt different courts and different judicial procedures according to these courts. In this regard, the arrangement is in order. Again, it is intended to be five years of maximum imprisonment, except for the Supreme Court's phase of the Supreme Court, in view of all crimes which have entered the field of duty, in accordance with the removal of the special official heavy criminal courts of the Anti-Terror Law on Article 10 of the Article 10 of the Penal Code on which the sentence will be applied twice as much as possible. The main thing is that people are being tried without arrest. As it is known, imprisonment is a measure that allows the evidence to be collected at ease in the event of the defendant's suspicion of escape, the danger of destroying criminal evidence. The proposed arrangement for this measure not to turn into a conviction is a step forward. The government's job is to make judgment fair and swift. In the slow work of the judicial body, the length of the trial process, the files that cannot be decided at the end, and the people who remain under arrest during that time, are a serious problem for our legal system in our country. Again, the proposal envisions significant changes in terms of democraticisation and guaranteeing basic rights and freedoms regarding the arrest measure. The amendment to Article 100 of the Penal Code can only be applied in the case of concrete evidence showing strong suspicion of crime. Dear lawmakers, again, the proposal has stated that the person arrested in the period of investigation and prosecution will be brought before the competent judge and court within 24 hours of the arrest warrant, which is known by the authorities for the arrest of the person caught in the court. If the person caught as innovation cannot be brought before a competent judge and court-martial within 24 hours, then the court will have the opportunity to question or testify by the competent judge or court, using audio and video communication system at the nearest courthouse, if not present. This is a very important arrangement in which basic rights and freedoms are guaranteed by combining technological possibilities and the legal system that have the ability to implement new applications in our legal system. As an example of this arrangement, if a person with a warrant from the Van court, for example, was arrested when he entered the Edirne border crossing, the current practice would have to be sent to the Van court on the subject of the person's transactions. This is an act that deprives a person of his freedom. Again, the arrangement will ensure that his testimony will be taken to the nearest court, where or where he was arrested, without being sent to the Van court on the subject, and if he does not need to be arrested, then he will not be deprived of his freedom and his job. This is one of the most important arrangements to be really careful about. While the current law requires reasonable doubt in implementing measures such as search and seizure, these measures are expected to be implemented in the case of strong doubt based on concrete evidence, with the amendment made by the proposal. In accordance with the proposal, the penalties for the privacy of private life are significantly increased for recording, unlawful possession, and not destroying personal data provided by everyone in the Turkish Penal Code in accordance with the right to protect personal data related to them. It is also organised that data not related to crime but obtained during the pursuit of crime should be destroyed, and if it is not destroyed, the punishments envisioned have been aggravated. The fourth article of Article 38 of the Constitution states that no one will be held guilty until the sentence is fixed. The right to innocence and not be tainted is also one of the universal principles of criminal law. This arrangement is indeed more advanced than the constitution of many advanced countries. Innocence is an important arrangement that everyone in the country needs and people have to be meticulous about each other. Taking that into consideration, the publicization of personal data during the criminal investigation will severely undermine these principles. This way, the explanation of false misinformation, data, can lead to very serious casualties. Therefore, by changing the terms and decision-making process of resorting to protection measures with the proposal and increasing the penalties for crimes committed against personal data, the right to innocence is more effective in the capacity and the right to remain unstained. Again, it is vital that the person held in Article 19 of the Constitution shall have freedom and security and the effective protection of property rights. In this context, protective measures have been brought to the movers by the amendment to Article 128 of the Penal Code, titled "Elkoyma" (the Penal Code), entitled "to those who are moving, rights, and to those who are to receive it." The catalog, which can be decided on protection measures related to listening, is removed from the substance text by offering to form an organisation to commit crimes. This is a subject that is suggested to undermine the state in the fight against organised crime, but it is an abstract organization crime removed from the text of matter. In the event of organised crime in the matter, the legislation will again be applied to the regulations envisioned by the law. The distinctions, considered the principle of equality of weapons, between the prosecution and the defence authorities, which would prevent the prosecution and defence action from being carried out as required, have been removed from the proposal of the law. When it was previously deemed necessary by the prosecutor's office, it was completely eliminated by the court of justice's decision to prevent the defendant from taking the necessary documents and to review the case. And that's actually a very important issue in defense. With the law proposal that I'm trying to make a general statement above, the goal of democratisation, guaranteeing basic rights and freedoms would be a very important step forward. I declare that we will vote in favour of the law that we believe to be in place, wish our nation well, and salute your high council.

tbmm-2014-02-20sit03spe0121par0006	Mr. President, I have taken a pledge on the first chapter of the First Amendment to the Supreme Criminal Courts established when Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law was warned. On this occasion, I salute the General Assembly. Dear lawmakers, the main goal of criminal law is to achieve material truth without causing human rights violations. The right to a fair trial stated in the European Convention on Human Rights, which we signed during criminal investigations and prosecutions, and the right to a fair trial in accordance with the Article 36 of our Constitution, each person has the right to a reasonable and reasonable trial in front of the judicial authorities, taking advantage of its legal means and means. The right to innocence, the right to silence, the right to balance and defense between what we call the equality of weapons and the right to defend are the most important principles the legal state requires to consider in criminal investigations and prosecutions. At the beginning of the debate over a fair trial in our country, the existence of state security courts has always been discussed. Military members of state security courts were removed in 1999, and state security courts were removed in 2004 during the AK PARTY administration. Penal Code 250 and Anti-Terror Law have continued its presence to date as a special official criminal court. In the legal state, the lawmaker has the authority to set rules on criminal justice and to make the arrangement the courts require, provided they are bound by the Constitutional rules on the establishment, duty and powers, functioning, judicial procedures and structure of the courts. As a result, the Constitution's 142th article on the matter clearly ruled that the establishment of the courts, its mandates, its functioning and its judicial procedures will be carried out by law. Mr. President, the high-profile lawmakers who continue their duties under Article 2 of Law 6352 with the proposal are being removed by Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law. This is a major reform for our country's legal history and for our criminal trial. Turkey will no longer have judicial institutions and investigative authorities, who are equipped with special powers, display a hierarchical view between courts, and are constantly criticised for their special investigation and prosecution procedures. As the most important of the efforts of the EC PARTI government to democratize since 2002, it will be a positive development for our human rights report card, which will be on the agenda of our Parliament. Given the international contracts and the basic principles in our Constitution, the Law on Anti-Terror Law and Turkish Penal Code contains positive amendments that predict the need for the procedure in case of disproportionateness, seeking a reasonable proportion of the damage, which is expected to be done with the benefits expected from the criminal trial. In this scope, changes in the detection, listening and recording of communication with the restructuring of some protection measures under the Penal Code are more appropriate for the safety of persons and the right to fair trial. With the changes made, the decision-making process for protection measures is being adopted with a more effective audit and the time for measures to be implemented is narrowed down. The definition of communication, listening, and recording are clearly stated in the law on what crimes to implement the measures of monitoring and confiscation of technical vehicles, the rights and the recipients. Among the crimes in which these measures will be carried out are the possibility of these measures being implemented in terms of crimes that are not in the catalog, using the substance referred to as a crime against the Turkish Criminal Code of 220. Because an important part of the crimes that an organization can commit in TCK 220 is already in the catalog. The increase in sentences brought under offer for the protection of privacy and protection of personal data is also appropriate changes. In the results report of the research commission on the detection of communications freedom and violations of privacy established by all party groups in the Grand National Assembly of Turkey last year, restrictions on the increase of penalties involved in the protection of personal data and the privacy of private life are entirely reflected in the commission report by all parties. Today, it is a positive change in the way that these ideas are carried out with a proposal of the law. Again, the fact that the concrete evidence criteria for applying for protection measures such as detention, arrest, search and seizure are being introduced, preventing the application of such measures due to abstract doubts and the damage of property rights and freedom of persons and security. So, what we're doing today is actually a historical arrangement, a historical change. The offer solves the problem. A matter that has been criticised for many years has been imposed as a five-year implementation of the maximum sentence for all crimes committed in the field of the severe criminal court, since the sentence on Article 10 of the Anti-Terror Law has been lifted from the scope of certain crimes. An important amendment to the proposal is the removal of restrictions on our legislation in terms of the defence's ability to review the case. A distinction between prosecutors and defence authorities that prevents the prosecution and defence action from being carried out according to the principle of equality of weapons is being provided by the defense to examine the file contents or sample the documents, allowing the defendant to examine the file contents without any restrictions on the investigation phase and to take a sample of the documents he wants. Dear lawmakers, the understanding of the legal state in terms of the state's commitment to the rule of law and justice has historically emerged after the understanding of the property state and police state, respectively. In the property state, the ruler provides his own authority and uses it without any rules. In other words, the property state has a full arbitrary regime. In the sense of the police state, the ruler is not bound by any rule. The rules of law in the police state, though not in any way binding the administration, are the rules that must be followed unconditionally for those governed. The understanding of the police state is, of course, shaken by the French Revolution and replaced by the understanding of the legal state. In the state of law, the procedures of every body of the state are adopted as long as it is in accordance with the constitution and the law and is organised within their framework. For this reason, the savings of state organs must be subject to judicial control. It is possible to talk about the two main democratisms that can prevent the arbitrary practices of organs that form state power. The first is public, media, and voter sensitivity. This power, determined as political and spiritual guarantees, is directly related to the country's level of democratisation. The second is measures to ensure that the state's bodies are in accordance with the constitution, the law, through judicial procedures. This assurance, declared legal guarantees, constitutes an effective guarantee that prevents state bodies from contracting unconstitutional savings.
